DESIGN.md

show
## Concept
The player pilots a tiny dot through an endless tunnel, using a tap‑activated bubble that briefly slows the world while the dot keeps its regular speed, creating a fleeting window to navigate tight obstacles. The bubble’s cooldown forces careful timing and strategic use.

## Player
The player controls a glowing dot that automatically moves forward through a scrolling tunnel. The player can steer left and right and tap to spawn a short‑lived time‑dilation bubble. The dot’s motion remains at normal speed at all times.

## Core loop
Every few seconds a new segment of the tunnel appears, populated with obstacles that rush toward the dot. The player watches the incoming pattern, decides whether to dodge by steering or to activate the bubble, and then reacts within the brief slow‑motion window. After the bubble expires a cooldown period begins, during which it cannot be used again, and the tunnel returns to full speed.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Sweeping walls** that slide horizontally across the tunnel, narrowing the passage.
- **Spinning blades** that rotate around a fixed point, sweeping a small arc.
- **Pulsing barriers** that expand and contract, momentarily blocking the path.
- **Static spikes** that jut from the tunnel floor or ceiling, requiring precise steering.
All hazards move consistently with the tunnel’s scrolling speed, disappearing once they pass the player.

## Score
Score increases each time the player successfully passes an obstacle segment. Bonus points are awarded for navigating a segment without using the bubble, rewarding skillful timing. Collectible orbs that appear occasionally grant extra points when grabbed.

## Win and lose
The player loses instantly on collision with any hazard, triggering a Game Over overlay. The player wins by surviving a predefined sequence of obstacle segments (e.g., completing a full tunnel cycle) or reaching a time threshold, after which a celebratory animation and score summary appear.

## Feel
Frantic yet rhythmic, with fast‑paced scrolling interrupted by brief pockets of slow motion that feel like a cinematic bullet‑time. Neon‑lit tunnel walls and glowing obstacles contrast against a dark background, accompanied by a pulsing synth soundtrack that accents the bubble activation.

## Why it's fun
The thrill of stretching a split‑second reaction into a slow‑motion window, letting the player dramatically dodge danger and feel mastery over time.

TASKS.md

show
1. HTML scaffold and meta.json — Create the HTML skeleton with required header, canvas, restart button, and stub script block; generate meta.json matching the header text.
2. Core game state objects — Define player, hazard list, score, phase, and bubble state as plain objects at the top of the script.
3. Main loop implementation — Set up requestAnimationFrame loop with delta‑time handling, cancel logic, and try/catch; clear the canvas each frame.
4. Input handling — Populate a `keys` Set via keyboard events, add tap detection for bubble activation, and bind R‑key and Restart button to a shared reset() function.
5. Player movement — Implement dt‑based left/right steering that updates player position and reflects changes in `window.__game`.
6. Time‑dilation bubble — Add tap‑activated bubble that temporarily slows world speed, enforce a cooldown period, and update observable state.
7. Hazard system — Create hazard data structure, spawn rules respecting SAFE_RADIUS, motion scaled by dt and affected by bubble slowdown.
8. Collision detection — Detect player‑hazard collisions, transition to "gameover" phase, stop the active gameplay loop, and trigger audio.
9. Scoring — Increase score on each passed obstacle segment, award bonus for bubble‑less segments, and draw the score HUD.
10. Rendering — Clear canvas, draw background, tunnel, hazards, player (high‑contrast), bubble overlay, and HUD each frame.
11. Game‑over overlay — Dim background and render "Game Over" with final score and restart instructions.
12. Observable contract — Populate `window.__game` each frame with `state`, `score`, `player` coordinates/visibility, and an objective sentence.
13. Audio — Lazy‑initialize AudioContext in a beep() helper and play sounds on hit, score, and death events.

    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Time Dilation</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Tap to create a short‑lived bubble where the tunnel and obstacles move in slow motion while the dot keeps its normal speed, giving a brief reaction window; a cooldown prevents constant use.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Tap to create a bubble</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
    // === STATE ==============================================================
    let player = { x: 400, y: 300 };
    let hazards = [];
    let score = 0;
    let phase = "menu";
    let bubble = { active: false, cooldown: 0 };
    let keys = new Set();

    // === INPUT ==============================================================
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
      if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
      keys.add(ev.key);
    });
    document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });

    document.getElementById("game").addEventListener("pointerdown", () => {
      if (bubble.cooldown <= 0) {
        bubble.active = true;
        bubble.cooldown = 2; // placeholder cooldown seconds
      }
    });

    document.getElementById("restart").addEventListener("click", reset);

    // Reset function stub
    function reset() {
      player = { x: 400, y: 300 };
      hazards = [];
      score = 0;
      phase = "menu";
      bubble = { active: false, cooldown: 0 };
      keys = new Set();
      publishState();
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
    function publishState() {
      window.__game = {
        state: phase,
        score,
        player: { x: player.x, y: player.y, visible: true },
        objective: "Tap to create a short‑lived bubble where the tunnel and obstacles move in slow motion while the dot keeps its normal speed."
      };
    }
    publishState();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

## Player

The player watches the incoming pattern, decides whether to dodge by steering or to activate the bubble, and then reacts within the brief slow‑motion window. After the bubble expires a cooldown period begins, during which it cannot be used again, and the tunnel returns to full speed.

## Hazards / Obstacles
- **Sweeping walls** that slide horizontally across the tunnel, narrowing the passage.
- **Spinning blades** that rotate around a fixed point, sweeping a small arc.
- **Pulsing barriers** that expand and contract, momentarily blocking the path.
- **Static spikes** that jut from the tunnel floor or ceiling, requiring precise steering.
All hazards move consistently with the tunnel’s scrolling speed, disappearing once they pass the player.

## Score

## Win and lose
The player loses instantly on collision with any hazard, triggering a Game Over overlay. The player wins by surviving a predefined sequence of obstacle segments (e.g., completing a full tunnel cycle) or reaching a time threshold, after which a celebratory animation and score summary appear.

`DESIGN.md` MUST contain these sections (use these exact headers; the validator checks for them):

## Concept
One or two sentences in your own words. What does the player do, and why is it satisfying?

## Player
Describe what the player controls — the avatar, ship, cursor, paddle, whatever it is. Visually, what is it (one sentence)? List its verbs (move, jump, shoot, place, type). Keep it abstract — no coordinates, no speeds.

## Core loop
What happens every 1–3 seconds of normal play. Be specific about the moment-to-moment experience: what's the player reading, what are they choosing between, what makes them lean forward.

## Hazards / Obstacles
What stands in the player's way. Describe each kind by behavior and threat — "fast horizontal sweepers that telegraph their path", "stationary blocks that explode when shot". Concepts and behaviors, not numbers.

## Score
What discrete events increase the score. Examples: "survived a wave", "collected a pickup", "killed an enemy at long range". Avoid score = elapsed time as the only mechanic.

## Win and lose
When does the player win? When do they lose? What happens on screen the moment it ends.

## Feel
The vibe. Pace (frantic / steady / meditative). Aesthetic direction (neon / pastel / monochrome / retro CRT). Sound character (chiptune / arcade beeps / silent). One short paragraph.

## Why it's fun
One sentence. The single moment that would make a player smile, curse, or shout. What is this game's hook?

Keep DESIGN.md focused on player experience. The next phase (tasks) will translate this into an implementation plan with concrete numbers.

The player can lose\n105: \n106: There must be a collision check (or other end condition) that fires `gameOver` and stops the active gameplay loop. \"Game runs forever, no death\" is broken. Examples that have failed in the past: collision check compared a value against itself; \"spike\" collision only clamped position instead of killing the player; obstacles never reached the player.\n107: \n108: ### 2. When spawning hazards, **reject any spawn that is within `SAFE_RADIUS` of the player AND would reach them within 3 seconds at current hazard speed**. Pre-spawning hazards on top of the player at t=0 has caused frame-1 deaths in past games. Don't do it.\n115: \n116: ### 4. The control actually changes the outcome\n117: \n118: Whatever the player presses must, in the next frame, change a value that affects collision or score. If a control is purely cosmetic (e.g. \"stretch horizontally\" but the gaps are vertical), the game is fundamentally broken even if it runs. Per-frame constants (`x += 5` every frame) make games run 2.4× faster on a 144Hz display.\n123: \n124: ```js\n125: let lastTime = performance.now();\n126: function loop(now) {\n127:   const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1); // seconds, clamped\n128:   lastTime = now;\n129:   // physics scaled by dt:\n130:   player.x += player.vx * dt;\n131:   enemy.y  += enemy.vy  * dt;\n132:   spawnTimer -= dt;\n133:   // ...\n134:   requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n135: }\n136: requestAnimationFrame(loop);\n137: ```\n138: \n139: ### 6. That function MUST `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` the previous loop before scheduling a new one — otherwise loops stack on each restart and the game slows to a crawl over time.\n142: \n143: ```js\n144: let loopId = null;\n145: function startLoop() {\n146:   if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);\n147:   function tick(now) { /* ... */ loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }\n148:   loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n149: }\n150: ```\n151: \n152: ### 7. Better to do as:

```
    // === AUDIO ==============================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "square";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }
```

Now Loop/Update:

```
    // === LOOP/UPDATE ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
```

Define update(dt) and render() functions.

Update function:

```
    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== "playing") return;
      // Player movement
      const dir = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0) -
                  (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
      // clamp
      player.x = Math.max(PLAYER_R, Math.min(canvas.width - PLAYER_R, player.x));

      // Bubble timer
      if (bubble.active) {
        bubble.timer -= dt;
        if (bubble.timer <= 0) {
          bubble.active = false;
          bubble.timer = 0;
        }
      }
      if (bubble.cooldown > 0) {
        bubble.cooldown -= dt;
        if (bubble.cooldown < 0) bubble.cooldown = 0;
      }

      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnHazard();
        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }

      // Move hazards
      const speed = bubble.active ? WORLD_BASE_SPEED * BUBBLE_FACTOR : WORLD_BASE_SPEED;
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        hz.y += speed * dt;
      }

      // Remove off-screen hazards and scoring
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const hz = hazards[i];
        // Collision detection
        if (hz.x < player.x + PLAYER_R && hz.x + hz.w > player.x - PLAYER_R &&
            hz.y < player.y + PLAYER_R && hz.y + hz.h > player.y - PLAYER_R) {
          phase = "gameover";
          beep(200, 0.3);
          // Stop loop after this frame; not cancel now to let render overlay
        }

        // Scoring when hazard passes player
        if (!hz.passed && hz.y > player.y) {
          hz.passed = true;
          // Score: 1 point, plus bonus if bubble not active during pass
          score += bubble.active ? 1 : 2; // give bonus when not slowed
          beep(600, 0.05);
        }

        // Remove if off canvas
        if (hz.y > canvas.height) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }
    }
```

    function spawnHazard() {
      // Determine safe spawn x
      let attempts = 0;
      let x;
      do {
        x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - HAZARD_W) + HAZARD_W/2;
        attempts++;
        if (attempts > 10) break;
      } while (Math.abs(x - player.x) < SAFE_RADIUS);
      const hz = { x: x - HAZARD_W/2, y: -HAZARD_H, w: HAZARD_W, h: HAZARD_H, passed: false };
      hazards.push(hz);
    }

    function render() {

```
    // === AUDIO ==============================================================
    function beep(freq, durSec) {
      try {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.type = "square";
        osc.frequency.value = freq;
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
        osc.start();
        osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      } catch {}
    }

    // === LOOP/UPDATE ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          update(dt);
          render();
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    function update(dt) {
      if (phase !== "playing") return;

      // Player movement
      const dir = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0) -
                  (keys.has("ArrowLeft") || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;
      player.x = Math.max(PLAYER_R, Math.min(canvas.width - PLAYER_R, player.x));

      // Bubble timers
      if (bubble.active) {
        bubble.timer -= dt;
        if (bubble.timer <= 0) {
          bubble.active = false;
          bubble.timer = 0;
        }
      }
      if (bubble.cooldown > 0) {
        bubble.cooldown -= dt;
        if (bubble.cooldown < 0) bubble.cooldown = 0;
      }

      // Spawn hazards
      spawnTimer -= dt;
      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {
        spawnHazard();
        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;
      }

      // Move hazards
      const speed = bubble.active ? WORLD_BASE_SPEED * BUBBLE_FACTOR : WORLD_BASE_SPEED;
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        hz.y += speed * dt;
      }

      // Collision and scoring
      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const hz = hazards[i];
        // Collision detection (point vs rect)
        if (
          player.x + PLAYER_R > hz.x &&
          player.x - PLAYER_R < hz.x + hz.w &&
          player.y + PLAYER_R > hz.y &&
          player.y - PLAYER_R < hz.y + hz.h
        ) {
          phase = "gameover";
          beep(200, 0.3);
        }

        // Scoring when passing player
        if (!hz.passed && hz.y > player.y) {
          hz.passed = true;
          score += bubble.active ? 1 : 2; // bonus when not slowed
          beep(600, 0.05);
        }

        // Cleanup offscreen
        if (hz.y > canvas.height) {
          hazards.splice(i, 1);
        }
      }
    }

    function spawnHazard() {
      // Ensure safe horizontal distance from player
      let attempts = 0;
      let x;
      do {
        x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - HAZARD_W);
        attempts++;
        if (attempts > 10) break;
      } while (Math.abs(x + HAZARD_W / 2 - player.x) < SAFE_RADIUS);
      const hz = { x: x, y: -HAZARD_H, w: HAZARD_W, h: HAZARD_H, passed: false };
      hazards.push(hz);
    }

    // === RENDER ==============================================================
    function render() {
      // Clear background
      ctx.fillStyle = "#0a0a0f";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

      // Draw hazards
      ctx.fillStyle = "#ff50aa";
      for (const hz of hazards) {
        ctx.fillRect(hz.x, hz.y, hz.w, hz.h);
      }

      // Draw player
      ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Bubble overlay
      if (bubble.active) {
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255,255,0,0.2)";
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, SAFE_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      // HUD
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.textAlign = "left";
      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
      ctx.textAlign = "right";
      ctx.fillText(`Phase ${phase}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

      // Game over overlay
      if (phase === "gameover") {
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        ctx.fillStyle = "#ff52ae";
        ctx.textAlign = "center";
        ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText("Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
        ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
        ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
        ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
        ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
      }
    }

    // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
```

1 : 0) -\n                  (keys.has(\"ArrowLeft\")  || keys.has(\"a\") || keys.has(\"A\") ? 1 : 0);\n      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;\n      // clamp\n      player.x = Math.max(PLAYER_R, Math.min(canvas.width - PLAYER_R, player.x));\n\n      // Bubble timer\n      if (bubble.active) {\n        bubble.timer -= dt;\n        if (bubble.timer <= 0) {\n          bubble.active = false;\n          bubble.timer = 0;\n        }\n      }\n      if (bubble.cooldown > 0) {\n        bubble.cooldown -= dt;\n        if (bubble.cooldown < 0) bubble.cooldown = 0;\n      }\n\n      // Spawn hazards\n      spawnTimer -= dt;\n      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n        spawnHazard();\n        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;\n      }\n\n      // Move hazards\n      const speed = bubble.active ? WORLD_BASE_SPEED * BUBBLE_FACTOR : WORLD_BASE_SPEED;\n      for (const hz of hazards) {\n        hz.y += speed * dt;\n      }\n\n      // Remove off-screen hazards and scoring\n      for (let i = hazards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n        const hz = hazards[i];\n        // Collision detection\n        if (hz.x < player.x + PLAYER_R && hz.x + hz.w > player.x - PLAYER_R &&\n            hz.y < player.y + PLAYER_R && hz.y + hz.h > player.y - PLAYER_R) {\n          phase = \"gameover\";\n          beep(200, 0.3);\n          // Stop loop after this frame; not cancel now to let render overlay\n        }\n\n        // Scoring when hazard passes player\n        if (!hz.passed && hz.y > player.y) {\n          hz.passed = true;\n          // Score: 1 point, plus bonus if bubble not active during pass\n          score += bubble.active ? 1 : 2; // give bonus when not slowed\n          beep(600, 0.05);\n        }\n\n        // Remove if off canvas\n        if (hz.y > canvas.height) {\n          hazards.splice(i, 1);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n```\n\nNeed spawnHazard function:\n\n```\n    function spawnHazard() {\n      // Determine safe spawn x\n      let attempts = 0;\n      let x;\n      do {\n        x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - HAZARD_W) + HAZARD_W/2;\n        attempts++;\n        if (attempts > 10) break;\n      } while (Math.abs(x - player.x) < SAFE_RADIUS);\n      const hz = { x: x - HAZARD_W/2, y: -HAZARD_H, w: HAZARD_W, h: HAZARD_H, passed: false };\n      hazards.push(hz);\n    }\n```\n\nNow render function:\n\n```\n    // === RENDER ==============================================================\n    function render() {\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#0a0a0f\";\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n\n      // Draw hazards\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff50aa\";\n      for (const hz of hazards) {\n        ctx.fillRect(hz.x, hz.h, hz.w, hz.h);\n        // Actually correct: rect at (hz.x, hz.y) with width w height h\n        ctx.fillRect(hz.x, hz.y, hz.w, hz.h);\n      }\n\n      // Draw player\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#7df9ff\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI*2);\n      ctx.fill();\n\n      // Bubble overlay\n      if (bubble.active) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(255,255,0,0.2)\";\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, SAFE_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI*2);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // HUD\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#e6e6f0\";\n      ctx.font = \"16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n      ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.fillText(`Phase ${phase}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);\n\n      if (phase === \"gameover\") {\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(10,10,15,0.7)\";\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff52ae\";\n        ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n        ctx.font = \"44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n        ctx.fillText(\"Game Over\", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 - 20);\n        ctx.font = \"20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"#e6e6f0\";\n        ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 14);\n        ctx.font = \"16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n        ctx.fillText(\"Press R or click Restart\", canvas.width/2, canvas.height/2 + 42);\n      }\n    }\n```\n\nBut we must be careful with variable names: In render we used hazard h incorrectly - 1 : 0) -\n                  (keys.has(\"ArrowLeft\") || keys.has(\"a\") || keys.has(\"A\") ? 1 : 0);\n      player.x += dir * PLAYER_SPEED * dt;\n      player.x = Math.max(PLAYER_R, Math.min(canvas.width - PLAYER_R, player.x));\n\n      // Bubble timers\n      if (bubble.active) {\n        bubble.timer -= dt;\n        if (bubble.timer <= 0) {\n          bubble.active = false;\n          bubble.timer = 0;\n        }\n      }\n      if (bubble.cooldown > 0) {\n        bubble.cooldown -= dt;\n        if (bubble.cooldown < 0) bubble.cooldown = 0;\n      }\n\n      // Spawn hazards\n      spawnTimer -= dt;\n      if (spawnTimer <= 0) {\n        spawnHazard();\n        spawnTimer = SPAWN_INTERVAL;\n      }\n\n      // Move hazards\n      const speed = bubble.active ? PLAYER_R < hz.x + hz.w &&\n          player.y + PLAYER_R > hz.y &&\n          player.y - PLAYER_R < hz.y + hz.h\n        ) {\n          phase = \"gameover\";\n          beep(200, 0.3);\n        }\n\n        // Scoring when passing player\n        if (!hz.passed && hz.y > player.y) {\n          hz.passed = true;\n          score += bubble.active ? 1 : 2; // bonus when not slowed\n          beep(600, 0.05);\n        }\n\n        // Cleanup offscreen\n        if (hz.y > canvas.height) {\n          hazards.splice(i, 1);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function spawnHazard() {\n      // Ensure safe horizontal distance from player\n      let attempts = 0;\n      let x;\n      do {\n        x = Math.random() * (canvas.width - HAZARD_W);\n        attempts++;\n        if (attempts > 10) break;\n      } while (Math.abs(x + HAZARD_W / 2 - player.x) < SAFE_RADIUS);\n      const hz = { x: x, y: -HAZARD_H, w: HAZARD_W, h: HAZARD_H, passed: false };\n      hazards.push(hz);\n    }\n\n    // === RENDER ==============================================================\n    function render() {\n      // Clear background\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#0a0a0f\";\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n\n      // Draw hazards\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff50aa\";\n      for (const hz of hazards) {\n        ctx.fillRect(hz.x, hz.y, hz.w, hz.h);\n      }\n\n      // Draw player\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#7df9ff\";\n      ctx.beginPath();\n      ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, PLAYER_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n      ctx.fill();\n\n      // Bubble overlay\n      if (bubble.active) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(255,255,0,0.2)\";\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(player.x, player.y, SAFE_RADIUS, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fill();\n      }\n\n      // HUD\n      ctx.fillStyle = \"#e6e6f0\";\n      ctx.font = \"16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n      ctx.textAlign = \"left\";\n      ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);\n      ctx.textAlign = \"right\";\n      ctx.fillText(`Phase ${phase}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);\n\n      // Game over overlay\n      if (phase === \"gameover\") {\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"rgba(10,10,15,0.7)\";\n        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        ctx.fillStyle = \"#ff52ae\";\n        ctx.textAlign = \"center\";\n        ctx.font = \"44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui\";\n        ctx.fillText(\"Game Over\", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - prompt (13,897 chars)
## Reference example: "breakout"

The following is a complete, validator-passing game. **Use it as a structural template** — your output must follow the same section ordering, the same patterns for the loop / restart / observable contract, and the same overall code style. Do NOT reproduce this game; you are building a different game described in the prompt below. The example exists only to anchor structure.

Notice in particular:
- The `// === SECTION ===` divider banners and their fixed order: CONFIG → STATE → RESET → LOOP/UPDATE → RENDER → INPUT → AUDIO → OBSERVABLE CONTRACT → BOOT.
- Plain object literals for game state (no `class`).
- A `keys` Set polled each frame instead of mutating game state from key handlers.
- `if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` at the top of `startLoop()`.
- The animation loop body wrapped in `try/catch`.
- `publishState()` called once per frame at the end of the tick.
- `window.__game` populated honestly (player.visible reflects actual viewport position).
- AudioContext initialized lazily inside `beep()`, wrapped in try/catch.

### Example `meta.json`
```json
{
  "title": "Neon Breaker",
  "description": "Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.",
  "controls": [
    "Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle",
    "Hold Space to launch faster",
    "Press R or click Restart"
  ],
  "tags": ["arcade", "breakout", "bouncing"]
}
```

### Example `index.html`
```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <title>Neon Breaker</title>
  <style>
    body { margin: 0; background: #0a0a0f; color: #e6e6f0; font-family: ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif; }
    .game-header { padding: 12px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #1d1d2a; }
    .game-header h1 { margin: 0; font-size: 18px; color: #7df9ff; }
    .game-header .game-description { margin: 4px 0; color: #a5a5b8; }
    .game-header .game-controls { margin: 4px 0; padding-left: 18px; color: #c8c8d8; }
    main { display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; padding: 16px; }
    canvas { background: #0f1320; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; }
    .game-restart { margin-top: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; background: #1c2030; color: #7df9ff; border: 1px solid #2a2f44; cursor: pointer; }
    .game-restart:hover { background: #232842; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header class="game-header">
    <h1>Neon Breaker</h1>
    <p class="game-description">Bounce the ball with the paddle and clear every brick. One ball, no second chances.</p>
    <ul class="game-controls">
      <li>Arrow keys or A/D to move the paddle</li>
      <li>Hold Space to launch faster</li>
      <li>Press R or click Restart</li>
    </ul>
  </header>
  <main>
    <canvas id="game" width="800" height="600"></canvas>
    <button id="restart" class="game-restart" type="button">Restart</button>
  </main>
  <script>
  // === CONFIG =============================================================
  const PADDLE_W = 110;
  const PADDLE_H = 14;
  const PADDLE_Y = 560;
  const PADDLE_SPEED = 700;       // px/sec

  const BALL_R = 7;
  const BALL_BASE_SPEED = 360;    // px/sec at start
  const BALL_MAX_SPEED  = 720;
  const BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT = 6; // px/sec added per brick

  const BRICK_ROWS = 6;
  const BRICK_COLS = 12;
  const BRICK_W = 60;
  const BRICK_H = 20;
  const BRICK_GAP = 4;
  const BRICK_TOP = 60;
  const BRICK_LEFT = (800 - (BRICK_COLS * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP) - BRICK_GAP)) / 2;
  const ROW_COLORS = ["#ff52ae", "#ff8c52", "#ffd23f", "#a3ff52", "#52ffae", "#7df9ff"];

  // === STATE ==============================================================
  const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
  const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");

  let paddle;        // { x } center x
  let ball;          // { x, y, vx, vy, speed }
  let bricks;        // array of { x, y, w, h, color, alive }
  let score;
  let bricksLeft;
  let phase;         // "playing" | "won" | "lost"
  let keys;          // pressed key set
  let highScore = 0;
  let lastTime;
  let loopId = null;
  let audioCtx = null;

  function reset() {
    paddle = { x: 400 };
    ball = { x: 400, y: 480, vx: 0, vy: 0, speed: BALL_BASE_SPEED };
    launchBall();
    bricks = [];
    for (let r = 0; r < BRICK_ROWS; r++) {
      for (let c = 0; c < BRICK_COLS; c++) {
        bricks.push({
          x: BRICK_LEFT + c * (BRICK_W + BRICK_GAP),
          y: BRICK_TOP + r * (BRICK_H + BRICK_GAP),
          w: BRICK_W, h: BRICK_H,
          color: ROW_COLORS[r % ROW_COLORS.length],
          alive: true,
        });
      }
    }
    bricksLeft = bricks.length;
    score = 0;
    phase = "playing";
    keys = new Set();
    lastTime = performance.now();
    publishState();
    startLoop();
  }

  function launchBall() {
    // Always launch upward, mild horizontal lean either side.
    const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + (Math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3);
    ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
    ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
    function tick(now) {
      try {
        const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
        lastTime = now;
        update(dt);
        render();
        publishState();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("loop error", err);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }

  // === UPDATE =============================================================
  function update(dt) {
    if (phase !== "playing") return;

    // Paddle.
    const dirX = (keys.has("ArrowRight") || keys.has("d") || keys.has("D") ? 1 : 0)
               - (keys.has("ArrowLeft")  || keys.has("a") || keys.has("A") ? 1 : 0);
    const speedMul = keys.has(" ") ? 1.6 : 1;
    paddle.x += dirX * PADDLE_SPEED * speedMul * dt;
    paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, PADDLE_W / 2, canvas.width - PADDLE_W / 2);

    // Ball motion.
    ball.x += ball.vx * dt;
    ball.y += ball.vy * dt;

    // Walls.
    if (ball.x < BALL_R) { ball.x = BALL_R; ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.x > canvas.width - BALL_R) { ball.x = canvas.width - BALL_R; ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx); beep(220, 0.04); }
    if (ball.y < BALL_R) { ball.y = BALL_R; ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy); beep(220, 0.04); }

    // Floor — lose.
    if (ball.y > canvas.height + BALL_R) {
      phase = "lost";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(110, 0.25);
      return;
    }

    // Paddle.
    const px = paddle.x, py = PADDLE_Y;
    if (ball.vy > 0
        && ball.y + BALL_R >= py
        && ball.y - BALL_R <= py + PADDLE_H
        && ball.x >= px - PADDLE_W / 2 - BALL_R
        && ball.x <= px + PADDLE_W / 2 + BALL_R) {
      // Bounce direction depends on where it hit on the paddle.
      const offset = (ball.x - px) / (PADDLE_W / 2); // -1..1
      const angle = (-Math.PI / 2) + offset * 1.0;   // up-ish, leaning side
      ball.vx = Math.cos(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = Math.sin(angle) * ball.speed;
      ball.y = py - BALL_R - 0.1;
      beep(540, 0.05);
    }

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      if (ball.x + BALL_R < b.x || ball.x - BALL_R > b.x + b.w) continue;
      if (ball.y + BALL_R < b.y || ball.y - BALL_R > b.y + b.h) continue;

      b.alive = false;
      bricksLeft--;
      score += 10;
      ball.speed = Math.min(BALL_MAX_SPEED, ball.speed + BALL_SPEEDUP_PER_HIT);

      // Decide which side was hit by comparing penetrations.
      const overlapX = Math.min(ball.x + BALL_R - b.x, b.x + b.w - (ball.x - BALL_R));
      const overlapY = Math.min(ball.y + BALL_R - b.y, b.y + b.h - (ball.y - BALL_R));
      if (overlapX < overlapY) ball.vx = -ball.vx; else ball.vy = -ball.vy;

      // Renormalize to current speed.
      const m = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
      ball.vx = (ball.vx / m) * ball.speed;
      ball.vy = (ball.vy / m) * ball.speed;
      beep(880, 0.05);
      break;
    }

    if (bricksLeft === 0) {
      phase = "won";
      if (score > highScore) highScore = score;
      beep(990, 0.2);
    }
  }

  // === RENDER =============================================================
  function render() {
    ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

    // Bricks.
    for (const b of bricks) {
      if (!b.alive) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.fillRect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h);
    }

    // Paddle.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#7df9ff";
    ctx.fillRect(paddle.x - PADDLE_W / 2, PADDLE_Y, PADDLE_W, PADDLE_H);

    // Ball.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, BALL_R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fill();

    // HUD.
    ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
    ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
    ctx.textAlign = "left";
    ctx.fillText(`Score ${score}`, 12, 22);
    ctx.textAlign = "right";
    ctx.fillText(`Bricks ${bricksLeft}  ·  Best ${highScore}`, canvas.width - 12, 22);

    if (phase === "lost" || phase === "won") {
      ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(10,10,15,0.7)";
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.textAlign = "center";
      ctx.font = "44px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillStyle = phase === "won" ? "#52ffae" : "#ff52ae";
      ctx.fillText(phase === "won" ? "You Win!" : "Game Over", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 20);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#e6e6f0";
      ctx.font = "20px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText(`Final score ${score}`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 14);
      ctx.fillStyle = "#a5a5b8";
      ctx.font = "16px ui-sans-serif, system-ui";
      ctx.fillText("Press R or click Restart", canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 42);
    }
  }

  // === INPUT ==============================================================
  document.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
    if (ev.key === "r" || ev.key === "R") { reset(); return; }
    keys.add(ev.key);
  });
  document.addEventListener("keyup", (ev) => { keys.delete(ev.key); });
  restartBtn.addEventListener("click", reset);

  // === AUDIO (lazy) =======================================================
  function beep(freq, durSec) {
    try {
      if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
      const gain = audioCtx.createGain();
      osc.type = "square";
      osc.frequency.value = freq;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, audioCtx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
      osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);
      osc.start(); osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + durSec);
    } catch {}
  }

  // === OBSERVABLE CONTRACT ===============================================
  function publishState() {
    window.__game = {
      state: phase === "playing" ? "playing" : "gameover",
      score,
      player: {
        x: paddle.x,
        y: PADDLE_Y + PADDLE_H / 2,
        visible: paddle.x >= 0 && paddle.x <= canvas.width,
      },
      objective: "Bounce the ball with the paddle to break every brick.",
    };
  }

  // === HELPERS ============================================================
  function clamp(v, lo, hi) { return v < lo ? lo : (v > hi ? hi : v); }

  // === BOOT ===============================================================
  reset();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

    // === CONFIG ==============================================================
    const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const restartBtn = document.getElementById("restart");
    let loopId = null;

    // === LOOP ==============================================================
    function startLoop() {
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      function tick(now) {
        try {
          const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
          lastTime = now;
          // Clear canvas each frame
          ctx.fillStyle = "#0f1320";
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          publishState();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("loop error", err);
        }
        loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      }
      loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    startLoop();
```

Inside, we add:

let loopId = null;
let lastTime = performance.now();

function startLoop() {
  if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
  function tick(now) {
    try {
      const dt = Math.min((now - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);
      lastTime = now;
      update(dt);
      // No render yet.
      publishState();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("loop error", err);
    }
    loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  loopId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

function update(dt) {
  // bubble cooldown
  if (bubble.cooldown > 0) {
    bubble.cooldown -= dt;
    if (bubble.cooldown <= 0) {
      bubble.active = false;
    }
  }

  // update hazards
  for (const h of hazards) {
    const speed = h.speed * (bubble.active ? 0.3 : 1);
    h.x += speed * dt; // move horizontally (assuming left to right)
  }

  // collision detection
  for (const h of hazards) {
    // Simple AABB collision (player as point)
    if (Math.abs(h.x - player.x) < h.w / 2 && Math.abs(h.y - player.y) < h.h / 2) {
      phase = "gameover";
      beep(110, 0.25);
      if (loopId !== null) cancelAnimationFrame(loopId);
      break;
    }
  }
}

Read CONVENTIONS.md and DESIGN.md in your working directory before doing anything else.

Your job in this stage is to break the implementation into a numbered list of small, ordered tasks. The next stage will work through this list one task at a time, in a single LLM session, reloading the page after each task and capturing console errors for you to fix.

Title: Time Dilation
Concept: Tap to create a short‑lived bubble where the tunnel and obstacles move in slow motion while the dot keeps its normal speed, giving a brief reaction window; a cooldown prevents constant use.

**You must produce one output file: `TASKS.md` in the working directory.** Use your write/edit tool to create this file on disk. After your turn ends, the validator runs `readFile("TASKS.md")` — if the file does not exist on disk, validation fails and your work is wasted. **Do NOT reply with the task list as inline chat text.** The list must be written to the file.

**Tool calls MUST be emitted as actual function/tool invocations — NOT described or simulated inside your reasoning/analysis text.** Writing tool-shaped JSON like `{"filePath":"TASKS.md","content":"..."}` *inside your chain-of-thought* does NOT invoke any tool. It is just text in the analysis channel that nothing reads or executes. The same goes for `{"pattern":"**/*"}` etc. — those only do something when emitted as a real `tool_use` event from the action channel, never when written into your thinking. Several prior runs have failed by hallucinating tool calls inside reasoning; do not repeat that pattern. If at any point you "describe" a tool call in your thinking instead of invoking it, you must immediately invoke the real tool afterward. The format MUST be a numbered list, one task per line, each line starting with `<number>.`:

```
1. <Task title> — <one-sentence description of what to do>
2. <Task title> — <one-sentence description>
...
```

The next stage parses tasks by matching `^\d+\.` at the start of each line. Don't use bullet sub-lists, don't number sub-tasks (1.1, 1.2). One number, one task, one line.

### What makes a good task

- **Small enough** to complete in a single coding turn (~50–150 lines of edits).
- **Testable**: after this task, what does the page do that it didn't before?
- **Leaves the file runnable**: at the end of the task, no syntax errors, no uncaught exceptions on load. Even if the game isn't playable yet, it must load.
- **Specific**: don't say "implement player". Say "implement player movement: arrow keys set keys.left/right; in update(), apply acceleration in the pressed direction with cap at MAX_SPEED, friction otherwise; clamp x to canvas bounds."
- **Concrete numbers**: this is where pixel sizes, ms intervals, and px/sec speeds live. Pick them. The implement stage will use them verbatim.

### Required coverage (in roughly this order)

Cover at minimum, ordered as below. Add tasks if the design demands more (powerups, level progression, screen wrap, etc):

1. **HTML scaffold + meta.json**: header (h1, description, controls list), canvas, restart button per CONVENTIONS.md. Stub `<script>` block (empty). meta.json matching header text.
2. **Game state objects**: player, hazards, score, phase using plain object literals at the top of the script. Set initial values.
3. **requestAnimationFrame loop**: startLoop() with `cancelAnimationFrame(loopId)` discipline at the top. Body wrapped in try/catch. Calls update(dt) and render(). Don't draw anything yet besides clearing the canvas.
4. **Input**: a `keys` Set populated by document keydown/keyup. R-key restart hook. Restart button click hook. Both call the same reset() function.
5. **Player movement**: dt-based physics tied to the key inputs from task 4. Verify each control listed in meta.json actually changes a value.
6. **Hazards**: data structure, spawn rules with a SAFE_RADIUS so nothing spawns on top of the player at t=0, motion (dt-scaled).
7. **Collision detection**: player vs hazards. On hit, transition phase to "gameover" and stop the active gameplay.
8. **Score**: discrete events that increase score, drawn on canvas at top-left.
9. **Render**: clear canvas, draw background, hazards, player, HUD. Player must be the highest-contrast object on screen.
10. **Game-over overlay**: dim background, "Game Over", final score, "Press R or click Restart".
11. **Observable contract**: populate `window.__game = { state, score, player:{x,y,visible}, objective }` every frame. `state` is "playing" | "gameover". `objective` is a 10–200 char single-sentence description.
12. **Audio**: lazy-init AudioContext inside a beep() helper, wrapped in try/catch. Hook into hit / score / death events.

You may merge two trivial tasks if they truly belong together (e.g. tasks 1+2 if state is just three lines). You may split a complex task in two. Aim for 10–15 tasks total.

**Final check before you stop**: confirm you actually called your write/edit tool with `TASKS.md` as the path. If you only described the task list in chat without invoking the tool, the file will not exist on disk and validation will fail. After the file is written, stop. Do not begin implementation.
